\cf0 COMMERCIAL-AUDIT should focus on whether the current Commercial baseline adequately handles Commercial distinctiveness, acting-as-presence/naturalism semantics, copy/tone/camera evidence, assessability-versus-performance boundaries, professional_presentation boundaries, no-brief grounding, anti-polish, anti-marketability, accessibility-safe language, live-room overclaim and display/label risks inside the existing architecture.\

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 17. Reusable Handoff Pack for COMMERCIAL-AUDIT\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\fs28 \cf0 COMMERCIAL-SYN handoff summary\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 COMMERCIAL-SYN is complete and establishes a source-grounded evidence base for auditing the current Commercial baseline. The strongest finding is that Commercial should not be treated as theatre acting or as generic screen acting. The B1 evidence supports Commercial as a distinct performance context centred on believable, relatable and grounded reality; task-calibrated tone; copy handling; and camera relationship. Direct-to-camera and off-camera-reader setups need different evidence expectations, and no-dialogue / silent-vignette Commercial work is a real but thinner sub-context.\
The B2 evidence supplies the self-tape assessability layer. Finished-tape Commercial feedback is only trustworthy when the performer can be clearly seen and heard, framing is task-appropriate, eyeline is controlled, the reader setup is legible, and the tape is continuous enough to judge the work. This batch strongly supports a separation between technical assessability and Commercial performance quality. Domestic capture, phones and simple setups can be acceptable; expensive equipment, studio polish, editing and professional packaging must not become Commercial merit.\
The B3 evidence gives the official fairness, safety and claim-scope boundary. Commercial audition requests should give enough information to ground task claims, self-tape burden should be proportionate, paid-reader or fee-based platform access should not become a hidden requirement, and nudity, stunt, privacy and consent boundaries are strong. Protected-characteristic and stereotype-led suitability claims are tightly constrained. Accessibility-safe Commercial assessment must treat access needs, visual impairment, Deaf/disabled access, fatigue, lighting, time-slot issues, accessible materials and reasonable adjustments as process and fairness matters, not deficits.\
B4 remains conditional only. It adds useful but narrow support for voiceover-style copy analysis through product cues, visual cues, cue words and point of view, but it does not solve UGC/social-style, presenter-led product or corporate/industrial Commercial subtypes. It should not outrank B1, B2 or B3.\
No audit recommendations have been created yet.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Compact COMMERCIAL-SYN-F finding list\
\pard\tx220\tx720\pardeftab720\li720\fi-720\partightenfactor0
\ls1\ilvl0
\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 \kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
COMMERCIAL-SYN-F01 \'97 Commercial is distinct from theatre acting.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
COMMERCIAL-SYN-F02 \'97 Commercial is distinct from generic screen acting where copy/product/campaign context matters.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
COMMERCIAL-SYN-F03 \'97 Mainstream Commercial performance is grounded, believable and relatable rather than exaggerated.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
COMMERCIAL-SYN-F04 \'97 Tone should be calibrated from supplied brief, product, campaign, copy or situation.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
COMMERCIAL-SYN-F05 \'97 Copy handling is a core Commercial evidence area.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
COMMERCIAL-SYN-F06 \'97 Product, brand or situation awareness requires supplied or observable context.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
COMMERCIAL-SYN-F07 \'97 Direct-to-camera Commercial work needs task-specific camera relationship and addressee logic.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
COMMERCIAL-SYN-F08 \'97 Reader-scene Commercial work is mediated and should not be judged as direct-address by default.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
COMMERCIAL-SYN-F09 \'97 No-dialogue / silent-vignette Commercial work is legitimate but thinner.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
COMMERCIAL-SYN-F10 \'97 Voiceover-style Commercial evidence is conditional only.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
COMMERCIAL-SYN-F11 \'97 UGC/social, presenter-led and corporate/industrial subtypes remain under-evidenced.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
COMMERCIAL-SYN-F12 \'97 Assessability is a precondition for reliable Commercial feedback.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
COMMERCIAL-SYN-F13 \'97 Framing and visibility are task-specific.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
COMMERCIAL-SYN-F14 \'97 Eyeline is task-specific.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
COMMERCIAL-SYN-F15 \'97 Reader access and reader quality are process issues, not talent evidence.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
COMMERCIAL-SYN-F16 \'97 Simple capture is acceptable; production polish is not Commercial merit.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
COMMERCIAL-SYN-F17 \'97 Technical/audio limits should narrow confidence before becoming performance criticism.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
COMMERCIAL-SYN-F18 \'97 Burden, deadlines, upload friction and admin are process issues, not talent evidence.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
COMMERCIAL-SYN-F19 \'97 Nudity, stunt, privacy and consent are process/safety boundaries.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
COMMERCIAL-SYN-F20 \'97 Protected-characteristic, appearance, marketability and bookability claims are tightly constrained.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
COMMERCIAL-SYN-F21 \'97 Accessibility-safe Commercial assessment requires proactive adjustment and non-deficit handling.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
COMMERCIAL-SYN-F22 \'97 Direction response, callback readiness and live-room adaptability must not be inferred from a finished tape.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
COMMERCIAL-SYN-F23 \'97 Generic Commercial praise requires observable replacement evidence.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
COMMERCIAL-SYN-F24 \'97 Source-shape limitations govern how evidence can be used.\

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Audit priority map\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 COMMERCIAL-AUDIT should prioritise: Commercial distinctiveness; acting-as-presence/naturalism semantics; suppression of generic \'93natural/confident/presence\'94 language; copy handling; tone calibration; direct-to-camera versus reader-scene handling; no-dialogue Commercial handling; no-brief brand/product/situation invention; production polish and equipment exclusion; reader-as-process support; assessability versus performance; audio/speech audibility; professional_presentation boundaries; live-room-only overclaim; accessibility-safe handling; anti-marketability and anti-appearance boundaries; subtype limitation handling; and display/label/comparison risks.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs28 \cf0 Baseline compatibility cautions\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 The synthesis supports wording, evidence-standard and report-behaviour improvements inside the current six-field architecture. It does not support score-field changes, weighting changes, cap/blocker/verdict changes, schema changes, pipeline changes or UI implementation proposals. Commercial acting-as-presence/naturalism can be clarified without changing the 
\f2\fs26 acting
\f1\fs24  field. Technical and audio fields should be treated as assessability/reliability supports. professional_presentation must be bounded to observable process, not polish or resources.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa280\partightenfactor0
